# PlateScreen -> Stride Sync Report — 2026-08-20

Automated weekly sync check. This report is output only — nothing under `C:\stride-app` was modified. All 240 candidates below are new to Stride (id not present in `sgFoodDb.ts`, `database/restaurants/`, or `database/grab-and-go/`, and not previously proposed in `_synced.json`).

## Codebase note

The sync task spec assumes three Stride target arrays (`SG_RESTAURANTS`, `SG_GRAB_AND_GO`, `SG_GROCERY`). The actual `app/src/lib/sgFoodDb.ts` only has **one** relevant array, `SG_RESTAURANTS` — grab-and-go chains (Grain, Starbucks, etc.) and grocery stores (FairPrice, Cold Storage, etc.) all live inside `SG_RESTAURANTS`, differentiated by `outletType`/`serviceTypes` fields, not by separate arrays. All snippets below target `SG_RESTAURANTS` regardless of the "intended" grouping noted per section.

## Summary

- **Candidates found:** 240 (of 299 non-home_cooked PlateScreen outlets; 59 already exist in Stride)
- **Ready to Apply:** 1 (`store_fairprice`)
- **Needs Admin Approval:** 239 (contain at least one `confidence: 'estimated'` item -> `source: 'ai_estimate'`)

### `store_fairprice` — FairPrice (17 items, tier: full_menu)

All 17 items are `confidence: 'verified'` (FairPrice ready-to-eat/grocery items, PlateScreen's synthetic grocery outlet — see `reference/migration-scripts/migrate.ts` groceries block). Target array: `SG_RESTAURANTS`.

**Naming collision warning:** Stride already has an outlet id  (FairPrice ready-to-eat counter items — rotisserie chicken, sushi, bento, etc., 8 items, ). This candidate, , is a *different* id from a different content set (17 raw grocery ingredients — chicken breast, oats, etc. — from PlateScreen's synthetic grocery-ingredients outlet). The id-based dedup check in Phase 1 correctly treats these as distinct (no id collision), but they represent the same real-world brand. Recommend the admin either (a) merge 's items into the existing  restaurant entry's menu instead of creating a second  card in the Eat page, or (b) rename this outlet (e.g. to "FairPrice Groceries") to visually distinguish it from the existing ready-to-eat  entry before applying.

### D. Named stalls in iconic hawker centres (90) — target: SG_RESTAURANTS

Maxwell Food Centre, Lau Pa Sat, Newton Food Centre, Chinatown Complex (`cc_*`), Tekka Market, Old Airport Road (`oar_*`), Golden Mile Food Centre (`gmfc_*`), Geylang Serai Market (`gsm_*`), Whampoa, and standalone icon Tian Tian Hainanese Chicken Rice. Some carry a real `sfa` block (licence number, SAFE grade — e.g. `tian_tian_chicken_rice`: licence `AMO094002`, grade A) from the 2026-08-09 SFA enhancement, which strengthens the *outlet* provenance, but macro data on every item is still `confidence: 'estimated'` — **no dedicated research-session doc** covers this batch (not in `reference/research-sessions/`), so treat macros as unverified pending review.

### E. "Whole hawker centre" generic entries (114) — target: SG_RESTAURANTS

Per README.md's SFA enhancement note: these are "'whole hawker centre' entries (many stalls, not one licence) — these get a centre-level record with `grade: 'not_applicable'` and no single `licenceNumber`". Each carries an **identical 8-item generic hawker-dish template** (not a real per-centre menu) — flag clearly to admin reviewers that these are placeholders representing "food is available here", not a verified menu for that specific centre. **No dedicated research-session doc** for this batch either.
